function Publish-Catalog($catalogKey, $tenantId, $companyId, $name, $domain, $itemsJson) {
  $uri = "http://localhost:3002/api/xbos/config-sync/catalog/$catalogKey/publish";
  $payload = @{ tenantId=$tenantId; companyId=$companyId; name=$name; domain=$domain; assignedTo=@('hrm','web-portal'); items=$itemsJson; actor='pm-seed' } | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 8;
  Invoke-RestMethod -Method Post -Uri $uri -Headers @{ 'x-internal-api-key'='xevn-dev-internal-key'; 'Content-Type'='application/json' } -Body $payload | Out-Null;
}
$profiles = @(
  @{ tenant='acme'; company='acme-hn'; legalName='ACME Logistics Vietnam' },
  @{ tenant='nova'; company='nova-hcm'; legalName='Nova Retail Group' }
);
foreach ($p in $profiles) {
  Publish-Catalog 'departments' $p.tenant $p.company 'Departments' 'organization' @(
    @{code='hr';label='Human Resources';status='active'},
    @{code='ops';label='Operations';status='active'},
    @{code='fin';label='Finance';status='active'},
    @{code='wh';label='Warehouse';status='active'}
  );
  Publish-Catalog 'positions' $p.tenant $p.company 'Positions' 'organization' @(
    @{code='hr_manager';label='HR Manager';status='active'},
    @{code='payroll_exec';label='Payroll Executive';status='active'},
    @{code='warehouse_supervisor';label='Warehouse Supervisor';status='active'},
    @{code='recruiter';label='Recruiter';status='active'}
  );
  Publish-Catalog 'insurance_providers' $p.tenant $p.company 'Insurance Providers' 'compliance' @(
    @{code='vbhx';label='Bao Hiem Xa Hoi Viet Nam';status='active'},
    @{code='pvi';label='PVI Insurance';status='active'},
    @{code='bao_viet';label='Bao Viet';status='active'}
  );
  Publish-Catalog 'contract_types' $p.tenant $p.company 'Contract Types' 'hr-policy' @(
    @{code='indefinite';label='Indefinite-term';status='active'},
    @{code='fixed_12m';label='Fixed-term 12 months';status='active'},
    @{code='probation_2m';label='Probation 2 months';status='active'}
  );
  Publish-Catalog 'legal_entities' $p.tenant $p.company 'Legal Entities' 'organization' @(
    @{code='head_office';label=$p.legalName;status='active'},
    @{code='branch_hcm';label=($p.legalName + ' - HCM Branch');status='active'}
  );

  Invoke-RestMethod -Method Post -Uri 'http://localhost:3001/api/hrm/settings-catalogs/sync-from-xbos' -Headers @{ 'x-internal-api-key'='xevn-dev-internal-key'; 'x-tenant-id'=$p.tenant; 'x-company-id'=$p.company; 'Content-Type'='application/json' } -Body '{}' | Out-Null;

  $extPayload = @{ items=@(@{ code='night_shift_allowance'; label='Night Shift Allowance'; status='active' }, @{ code='oncall_bonus'; label='On-call Bonus'; status='active' }) } | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 8;
  Invoke-RestMethod -Method Post -Uri 'http://localhost:3001/api/hrm/settings-catalogs/positions/extension-items' -Headers @{ 'x-internal-api-key'='xevn-dev-internal-key'; 'x-tenant-id'=$p.tenant; 'x-company-id'=$p.company; 'Content-Type'='application/json' } -Body $extPayload | Out-Null;
}
Write-Output 'Seeded XBOS catalogs + synced HRM for tenants: acme/acme-hn, nova/nova-hcm';`

def nodePrint(String expr) {
  def out = ["node", "--print", expr].execute(null, rootDir).text.trim()
  def realRoot = System.getenv("GRADLE_REAL_REPO_ROOT")
  def substRoot = System.getenv("GRADLE_SUBST_REPO_ROOT")
  if (realRoot != null && substRoot != null && out.contains(realRoot)) {
    return out.replace(realRoot, substRoot)
  }
  return out
}

def nodeFile(String expr) {
  return new File(nodePrint(expr))
}

def rnGradlePluginBuildDir = {
  def rnGradleLink = new File(settingsDir, ".rn-gradle-plugin")
  if (rnGradleLink.exists()) {
    return rnGradleLink
  }
  return nodeFile("require.resolve('@react-native/gradle-plugin/package.json', { paths: [require.resolve('react-native/package.json')] })").getParentFile()
}()

pluginManagement {
  def version = providers.exec {
    commandLine("node", "-e", "console.log(require('react-native/package.json').version);")
  }.standardOutput.asText.get().trim()
  def (_, reactNativeMinor, reactNativePatch) = version.split("-")[0].tokenize('.').collect { it.toInteger() }

  includeBuild(new File(settingsDir, "react-settings-plugin"))

  includeBuild(rnGradlePluginBuildDir)
}

plugins { id("com.facebook.react.settings") }

def getRNMinorVersion() {
  def version = providers.exec {
    commandLine("node", "-e", "console.log(require('react-native/package.json').version);")
  }.standardOutput.asText.get().trim()

  def coreVersion = version.split("-")[0]
  def (major, minor, patch) = coreVersion.tokenize('.').collect { it.toInteger() }

  return minor
}

if (getRNMinorVersion() >= 75) {
  extensions.configure(com.facebook.react.ReactSettingsExtension) { ex ->
    if (System.getenv('EXPO_UNSTABLE_CORE_AUTOLINKING') == '1') {
      println('\u001B[32mUsing expo-modules-autolinking as core autolinking source\u001B[0m')
      def command = [
        'node',
        '--no-warnings',
        '--eval',
        'require(require.resolve(\'expo-modules-autolinking\', { paths: [require.resolve(\'expo/package.json\')] }))(process.argv.slice(1))',
        'react-native-config',
        '--json',
        '--platform',
        'android'
      ].toList()
      ex.autolinkLibrariesFromCommand(command)
    } else {
      ex.autolinkLibrariesFromCommand()
    }
  }
}

rootProject.name = 'XeVN HRM'

dependencyResolutionManagement {
  versionCatalogs {
    reactAndroidLibs {
      from(files(new File(nodePrint("require.resolve('react-native/package.json')"), "../gradle/libs.versions.toml")))
    }
  }
}

apply from: new File(nodePrint("require.resolve('expo/package.json')"), "../scripts/autolinking.gradle");
useExpoModules()

if (getRNMinorVersion() < 75) {
  apply from: new File(nodePrint("require.resolve('@react-native-community/cli-platform-android/package.json', { paths: [require.resolve('react-native/package.json')] })"), "../native_modules.gradle");
  applyNativeModulesSettingsGradle(settings)
}

include ':app'
includeBuild(rnGradlePluginBuildDir)
'@
Set-Content -Path "android\settings.gradle" -Value $sg -Encoding UTF8NoBOM
Write-Host "Patched settings.gradle"`

  def substRoot = System.getenv("GRADLE_SUBST_REPO_ROOT")

  if (substRoot == null || realRoot == null) {

  def dirPath = project.projectDir.absolutePath
  if (dirPath.startsWith(realRoot)) {

      project.projectDir = new File(substRoot + rel)
    } else {
      project.projectDir = new File(substRoot + File.separator + rel)
    }
  }
}

node -e @"
const fs=require('fs');
const path=require('path');
const p=require.resolve('expo-modules-autolinking/package.json');
const g=path.join(path.dirname(p),'scripts/android/autolinking_implementation.gradle');
let t=fs.readFileSync(g,'utf8');
const helper = `
def mapGradleSubstPath(String p) {
  if (p == null) return p
  def realRoot = System.getenv('GRADLE_REAL_REPO_ROOT')
  def substRoot = System.getenv('GRADLE_SUBST_REPO_ROOT')
  if (realRoot == null || substRoot == null) return p
  if (p.contains(realRoot)) return p.replace(realRoot, substRoot)
  def marker = 'xevn-ecosystem'
  def idx = p.toLowerCase().indexOf(marker.toLowerCase())
  if (idx < 0) return p
  def rel = p.substring(idx + marker.length())
  if (rel.startsWith(File.separator)) rel = rel.substring(File.separator.length())
  else if (rel.startsWith('/')) rel = rel.substring(1)
  return new File(substRoot, rel).absolutePath
}
`;
if (!t.includes('mapGradleSubstPath')) {
  t = t.replace('class ExpoModuleGradleProject {', helper + '\nclass ExpoModuleGradleProject {');
}
t = t.replace(
  'project(\":\${moduleProject.name}\").projectDir = new File(moduleProject.sourceDir)',
  'project(\":\${moduleProject.name}\").projectDir = new File(mapGradleSubstPath(moduleProject.sourceDir))'
);
t = t.replace(
  'includeBuild(new File(modulePlugin.sourceDir))',
  'includeBuild(new File(mapGradleSubstPath(modulePlugin.sourceDir)))'
);
fs.writeFileSync(g,t);
console.log('patched', g);
"@
# Remove projectsLoaded block from settings.gradle
$settings = "android\settings.gradle"
$s = Get-Content $settings -Raw
$s2 = $s -replace "(?s)\r?\ngradle\.projectsLoaded \{[^}]+\}\r?\n\r?\n", "`n"
if ($s -ne $s2) { Set-Content $settings $s2 -NoNewline; "removed projectsLoaded" } else { "no projectsLoaded block" }`

if (c.includes('GRADLE_PATH_EXPO_CLI')) {
  console.log('expo cli env already in gradle.cjs');
} else {
  const needle = '  env.GRADLE_RN_CLI_BIN = toSubstPath(cliBin);';
  const add = needle + `
  const expoCli = require.resolve('@expo/cli', { paths: [path.dirname(expoPkg), mobileRoot, repoRoot] });
  env.GRADLE_PATH_EXPO_CLI = toSubstPath(expoCli);
  const codegenPkg = require.resolve('@react-native/codegen/package.json', {
    paths: [path.dirname(rnPkg), mobileRoot, repoRoot],
  });
  env.GRADLE_PATH_CODEGEN_DIR = toSubstPath(codegenPkg);
  const entry = require('child_process').execSync(
    ['node', '-e', "process.stdout.write(require('expo/scripts/resolveAppEntry')(process.argv[1], process.argv[2], process.argv[3]))", mobileRoot, 'android', 'absolute'].map((a) => `"${a}"`).join(' '),
    { cwd: mobileRoot, encoding: 'utf8', shell: true },
  ).trim();
  env.GRADLE_PATH_APP_ENTRY = toSubstPath(entry);`;
  if (!c.includes(needle)) { console.error('needle missing'); process.exit(1); }
  c = c.replace(needle, add);
  fs.writeFileSync(p, c);
  console.log('patched gradle.cjs expo cli env');
}

const appGradle = path.join(__dirname, '..', 'android', 'app', 'build.gradle');
let g = fs.readFileSync(appGradle, 'utf8');
if (!g.includes('GRADLE_PATH_APP_ENTRY')) {
  const old = "entryFile = file([\"node\", \"-e\", \"require('expo/scripts/resolveAppEntry')\", projectRoot, \"android\", \"absolute\"].execute(null, rootDir).text.trim())";
  const neu = 'def entryEnv = System.getenv("GRADLE_PATH_APP_ENTRY")\n    entryFile = entryEnv != null ? file(entryEnv) : file(["node", "-e", "require(\'expo/scripts/resolveAppEntry\')", projectRoot, "android", "absolute"].execute(null, rootDir).text.trim())';
  if (!g.includes(old)) { console.error('entry line missing'); process.exit(1); }
  g = g.replace(old, neu);
  fs.writeFileSync(appGradle, g);
  console.log('patched app build.gradle entry');
}

const path = require('path');
const { getDefaultConfig } = require('expo/metro-config');

const projectRoot = process.env.REACT_NATIVE_PACKAGER_ROOT || __dirname;

  console.log("gradle.cjs already patched");
  process.exit(0);
}

const winShortFn = `
function winShortPath(abs) {
  if (process.platform !== "win32") return abs;

    const quoted = abs.replace(/'/g, "''");

      .execSync(
        \`powershell -NoProfile -Command "(New-Object -ComObject Scripting.FileSystemObject).GetFolder('\${quoted}').ShortPath"\`,

  } catch {

  }
}

JUNCTION_MOBILE_ROOT\n  : path.resolve(path.join(__dirname, '..'));",
  );

    "const repoRoot = path.resolve(path.join(mobileRoot, '..', '..', '..'));",

  );
}

const junctionSubst = `function resolveExecCwd() {
  const forceJunctionSubst =
    isWin && repoRoot === JUNCTION_REPO_ROOT && process.env.GRADLE_JUNCTION_SUBST !== "0";
  if (!forceJunctionSubst && (!isWin || !pathHasNonAscii(repoRoot))) {
    return { execCwd: androidDir, substDrive: null };
  }
  const drive = substRepoDrive(repoRoot);
  if (forceJunctionSubst) {
    console.error("[gradle] Junction repo â†’ subst", drive, "â†’", repoRoot);
  } else {
    console.error("[gradle] Unicode path â†’ subst", drive, "â†’", repoRoot);
  }
  const relAndroid = path.relative(repoRoot, androidDir);
  const execCwd = path.resolve(\`\${drive}\\\\\`, relAndroid);
  if (!fs.existsSync(path.join(execCwd, isWin ? "gradlew.bat" : "gradlew"))) {
    substDelete(drive);
    throw new Error(\`[gradle] KhĂ´ng tháº¥y gradlew táº¡i: \${execCwd}\`);
  }
  console.error("[gradle] cwd Gradle:", execCwd);
  return { execCwd, substDrive: drive };
}`;

c = c.replace(/function resolveExecCwd\(\) \{[\s\S]*?return \{ execCwd, substDrive: drive \};\n\}/, junctionSubst);

const toJunctionFn = `
function toJunctionPath(absPath) {
  if (!absPath) return absPath;
  const marker = "xevn-ecosystem";
  const lower = absPath.toLowerCase();
  const idx = lower.indexOf(marker.toLowerCase());
  if (idx < 0) return absPath;
  let rel = absPath.slice(idx + marker.length);
  if (rel.startsWith(path.sep)) rel = rel.slice(path.sep.length);
  else if (rel.startsWith("/")) rel = rel.slice(1);
  return path.join(JUNCTION_REPO_ROOT, rel);
}

function gradlePath(absPath) {
  return substDrive ? toSubstPath(absPath) : toJunctionPath(absPath);
}
`;

if (!c.includes("function gradlePath")) {

}

const envBlockEnd = c.indexOf("  if (substDrive) {");

const newEnv = `  env.GRADLE_RN_PLUGIN_ROOT = gradlePath(path.dirname(rnGradle));

  env.GRADLE_PATH_EXPO_MODULES_CORE_DIR = gradlePath(path.join(path.dirname(expoCore), "android"));
  env.GRADLE_PATH_RN_DIR = gradlePath(rnDir);
  env.GRADLE_PATH_JSC_DIST = gradlePath(path.join(path.dirname(jscPkg), "dist"));
  env.GRADLE_RN_CLI_BIN = gradlePath(cliBin);
  env.GRADLE_PATH_EXPO_CLI = gradlePath(expoCli);
  env.GRADLE_PATH_CODEGEN_DIR = gradlePath(path.dirname(codegenPkg));
  env.GRADLE_MOBILE_ROOT = gradlePath(mobileRoot);
  env.GRADLE_PATH_APP_ENTRY = gradlePath(path.join(mobileRoot, "index.ts"));

  const localRnGradleLink = path.join(mobileRoot, "android", ".rn-gradle-plugin");
  if (fs.existsSync(localRnGradleLink)) {
    env.GRADLE_RN_PLUGIN_ROOT = path.resolve(localRnGradleLink);
    env.GRADLE_RN_PLUGIN_REL = path
      .relative(execCwd, env.GRADLE_RN_PLUGIN_ROOT)
      .split(path.sep)
      .join("/");
  }

`;
c = c.slice(0, envBlockStart) + newEnv + c.slice(envBlockEnd);

c = c.replace(
  /if \(substDrive\) \{[\s\S]*?env\.EXPO_PROJECT_ROOT = pack;\n  \}/,

    const substRootAbs = path.resolve(\`\${substDrive}\\\\\`);

    env.GRADLE_RN_PLUGIN_REL = path

      .split(path.sep)
      .join("/");
    const pack = toSubstPath(mobileRoot);
    env.REACT_NATIVE_PACKAGER_ROOT = pack;
    env.PROJECT_ROOT = pack;
    env.EXPO_PROJECT_ROOT = pack;
  }`,
);

const https=require("https");
const zlib=require("zlib");

const j=execSync("npx eas-cli@latest build:view 97106266-7bbb-44f0-a6d0-bd0533b9831c --json",{encoding:"utf8"});

  res.on("end",()=>{
    let b=Buffer.concat(chunks);
    console.log("raw bytes",b.length,"hex",b.slice(0,8).toString("hex"));

    console.log("indices",i,j2,start);
    const excerpt=t.slice(start>=0?start:Math.max(0,t.length-12000));
    const lines=excerpt.split(/\n/);
    console.log("---EXCERPT---\n"+lines.slice(-60).join("\n"));
  });
});

      ["inflate", () => zlib.inflateSync(b)],
      ["inflateRaw", () => zlib.inflateRawSync(b)],
      ["unzip", () => zlib.unzipSync(b)],
    ];
    let text = null;
    for (const [name, fn] of tryFns) {
      try {
        const out = fn();
        console.log("OK", name, "->", out.length);
        text = out.toString("utf8");
        break;
      } catch (e) {
        /* */
      }
    }
    if (!text) {
      text = b.toString("utf8");
      console.log("fallback utf8 len", text.length, "printable ratio", (text.match(/[\x20-\x7E\n\r\t]/g)||[]).length/text.length);
    }
    const outPath = process.env.TEMP + "/eas-971-full.txt";
    fs.writeFileSync(outPath, text);
    const markers = ["FAILURE: Build failed", "FAILURE:", "What went wrong", "BUILD FAILED in", "Run gradlew"];
    for (const m of markers) console.log(m, text.indexOf(m));
    const idx = Math.max(...markers.map((m) => text.lastIndexOf(m)));
    const start = idx >= 0 ? idx : Math.max(0, text.length - 15000);
    const lines = text.slice(start).split(/\r?\n/);
    console.log("---LAST 60 LINES FROM FAILURE---\n" + lines.slice(-60).join("\n"));
  });
});
